| S-831 | code | Gamma-space compositing | Retail (D3D9, no sRGB conversion) modulates textures and blends the frame buffer on the gamma-encoded values the display shows: white ALPHA at alpha 0.25 over black displays 0.25. Godot's Forward+ colour buffer is linear, which displayed 0.5333 (RENDER-3 review) and made the white construction dust (BuildingContructDust: RGB 255, Shader ALPHA, Alpha2 0.15..0.35) far too opaque. Ported (lane RENDER-3): GodotGammaComposite's two CompositorEffects on the active camera sRGB-encode the RGBA16F colour buffer before the transparent pass and decode it after; the transparent-pass shaders hand over gamma-space values (FX particles with raw texels x raw diffuse, streaks, blended W3D surfaces, roads / rivers / standing water at output_gamma 1); the opaque pass and the alpha-test particles keep one conversion; retail's colour and alpha data are unchanged. Measured: 0.2471 (the alpha byte 63). Review r2: with MSAA the encode rewrites every sample of the multisample attachment the transparent pass draws into (a copy read per sample by a sample-shaded full-screen pass; the decode stays on the resolved layer), so MSAA 2x / 4x keep an opaque grey 0.5 at 0.4980 and show white 0.25 over it as 0.6235 (previously 0.2157 / 0.4118); if that pass cannot run, ensure() turns the camera's viewport to MSAA off and reports why. MapTerrainBuilder's map root is a GammaCompositeHost and the road / river / standing water materials get output_gamma 1 when they are built (no longer from GameWorld's shroud refresh). NOT reproduced (reported, pinned): D3D9 saturates every blend result to [0, 1], the RGBA16F buffer does not, so additive overbright survives later alpha / multiply draws (particles, streaks, additive W3D overlaps): additive red + additive red + black at 0.5 shows (1, 0, 0), retail about (0.5, 0, 0); white + additive red + multiply grey 0.5 shows (1, 0.502, 0.502), retail about (0.5, 0.5, 0.5); a fix must saturate between draws (a clamp after the pass leaves both wrong). Review r3: the default MSAA encode reads the resolved opaque image (access_resolved_color) once per pixel and writes its gamma encoding to all samples (no attachment copy, no per-sample shading); interior pixels are exact, an opaque geometry EDGE keeps Godot's linear-space sample average (half-covered grey 0.8 over 0.5 shows 0.6706) where retail averages displayed values (0.65), reported as an S-831 line; the project setting openbfme/rendering/gamma_exact_msaa_edges selects the exact per-sample encode (shows 0.6471). Framebuffers, pipelines and sample copies are owned per attachment / format and released (two simultaneous 2x / 4x viewports create none in 600 frames). Review r4: a per-frame prune on the render thread (frame_post_draw -> call_on_render_thread, bound to the encode effect, disconnected at shutdown) retires orphaned entries whether or not an MSAA viewport remains: switching the exact mode off, turning MSAA off or deleting the last MSAA viewport frees the sample copies within a few frames (gamma_composite_test _case_retirement). Cost (AMD NAVI33, paused Evendim scene, 102 objects, GPU timestamps, 144-fps cap): review r2 with MSAA disabled, +0.185 ms/frame at 1440p and +0.997 ms/frame at 4K; review r3 with 4x MSAA and the per-sample encode, +2.10-2.11 ms/frame at 1440p and +4.23-4.25 ms/frame at 4K, total GPU time about 4.06-4.08 ms / 8.20-8.23 ms (4K / 4x missed the 6.944 ms budget for 144 fps). Lane RENDER-3 r3 (same scene, medians, the two modes measured back to back): default encode 2x +0.51 ms (1440p, total 2.17) / +1.40 ms (4K, total 4.81), 4x +0.60 ms (1440p, total 2.57) / +1.59 ms (4K, total 5.57: within the 144-fps budget); exact per-sample encode 2x +1.33 / +3.21 ms, 4x +2.38 / +4.90 ms (4K total 8.89). Busy-battle and Steam Deck APU performance are not established. INFERENCE (the remainder this stop keeps): the half-float round trip, the sRGB curve as the display gamma, Godot-built transparent materials that are not ours (tool scenes only), and a colour buffer of another format (the effect then reports itself) | `S-831:` lines of FXPlayer::get_unverified (GammaComposite::unverified), in GameWorld::get_fx_report().stops | `fx_render_test.gd` _case_gamma_alpha (0.25 +- 1/255 and the S-831 line) and _case_saturation (the two pinned reproductions and the exact saturation line), `gamma_composite_test.gd` (MSAA off / 2x / 4x over grey, terrain only and terrain + W3DInstancer), `render3 gamma pass: a blended W3D surface*` (test_render3_construction.cpp) | the exact display curve and precision of retail compositing | a retail frame capture of a known particle over a known background |

Capture-flag presentation update (S-090): `LiveScripting` delivers authored
condition-state enter/leave LuaEvents for CAPTUREFLAG objects on the logic
owner immediately before each client model-condition flush when the selected
condition state changes, independently of the AI update schedule. This runs the mounted generic
handler, including its faction-subobject selection, without render-driven
simulation callbacks. It is an inferred timing bridge: animation-frame and
script-selected transition events remain unported. The runtime reports the
exact S-090 limitation. Owner/capturer sides come from the live players; client
flush events carry the capturer’s colour while raising and the owner’s after
cancellation or transfer. Regression: `capture flag presentation: ...` in
`test_hero_retail.cpp`.
